HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B001","root_001583/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_04a7f2e1da9a51ab8d22","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:12:3\",\"root_001583\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Doğru yolu göstermek, iki ayrı kolaylaştırmanın arasında yalnızca bilgi vermek değil, izlenebilir doğru güzergâhı belirlemek anlamı kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1638a62ff80c7294c532","text":"2. **Guidance as course and direction.** Evidence: `ه د ي` B002 (course/manner/intended direction), activated by 92:7/10 two facilitated routes. Structural cue: two paths have just been made easy. Mechanism: the word هدى gives a directional grammar to both routes and their divergence. Abductive move: I infer that guidance names the true course by which facilitation should be read. Reading change: the ayah clarifies that the split paths are not symmetrical in value.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1ea756962d2f13969732","text":"92:13–14 activated `ه د ي:B003` more strongly than the initial route contrast did: temporal scope and warning make guidance a marker placed ahead of consequence. Turning in 92:16 and protective removal in 92:17 then confirm that the marker organizes opposed spatial outcomes.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2e3fb46f3e233f5007b8","text":"92:20’s “face of his Lord, the Highest” made the day’s disclosure vertical and directional, not merely bright. The bridal display/gift branches also became less ornamental after 92:19 removed repayable favor; display, gift, and face all became covenantal exposure before God.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8410e61787999af006fe","text":"1. **Guidance as an actual course through the two facilitations.** `ٱلْهُدَىٰ` anchors `ه د ي:B001` (gentle indication toward way or truth) and `ه د ي:B002` (course, manner, and intended direction). The window contains facilitation toward ease in 92:7, facilitation toward hardship in 92:10, and turning away in 92:16. These spatially organized alternatives activate guidance as a traversable direction rather than information alone. The packet supplies paths and turning; the abductive arrow is that guidance identifies and enables the proper course among them. The emphatic construction consequently reads as an undertaking to furnish direction within a field of formed trajectories.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8f7de226b8a3809e39c7","text":"1. **Guidance as divine obligation of indication.** Evidence: `ه د ي` B001 (gentle guidance toward way/truth), activated by the explicit “upon Us.” Structural cue: after the false self-sufficiency fails, the speaker states what belongs to divine responsibility. Mechanism: guidance is a supplied road-sign and enabling, not a commodity bought by wealth. Abductive move: I infer that “upon Us” marks gracious self-binding rather than external compulsion. Reading change: the ayah becomes a mercy-pledge in the midst of judgment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a161b1212da5806027b2","text":"2. **Guidance goes ahead of consequence.** `ه د ي:B003` supplies the leading front part or guide that precedes. The reverse temporal pair “the later and the first” in 92:13, warning in 92:14, and eventual removal from danger in 92:17 place `ٱلْهُدَىٰ` before the consequences it makes visible. The inference is that prior indication functions like a leading edge which consequence follows; chronology is supplied, but this functional role is abductive. Guidance thus reads as advance placement of a marker, not a direction disclosed only after arrival.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B004","root_001583/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ba8151ce9a2532d503f8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:12:3\",\"root_001583\",[\"B004\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"İnsanların vermesi bağlamında yol gösterme, karşılık beklenmeden sunulan ilk armağan ve sunu olarak da okunabilir; bu dolaşım ayette doğrudan kurulmaz.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c40e3d80c713bd3d3b2a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:12:3\",\"root_001583\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sonuçlardan önce gelen yönlendirme, tehlike ve korunma ortaya çıkmadan önce yerleştirilmiş bir öncü işaret gibi çalışır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e5d313cc60f2197ac336","text":"3. **Guidance as gift/offering.** Evidence: `ه د ي` B004 (gracious gift), B005 (offering sent to sanctuary), activated by giving in 92:5 and 92:18-20. Structural cue: divine guidance stands between human giving and final recompense. Mechanism: the first gift is divine orientation; human giving imitates it downstream. Abductive move: I infer gift-circulation from shared giving roots, not surface identity. Reading change: guidance reads as the original non-transactional gift.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
